Keep existing profile for JDK 8 compatibility\n2. Add new profile for JDK 9+ that sets toolsjar to ${java.home}/lib/jrt-fs.jar\n   (a dummy jar that exists in JDK 9+)\n3. Since tools.jar dependency has scope=\"system\", Maven won't actually try to\n   use it at runtime - the compiler API is available through the module system\n\nAlternative: We could also exclude the tools dependency from compile-testing,\nbut that might break the test compilation if compile-testing expects it.\n\nThe safest fix is to provide a valid systemPath that points to an existing jar\nfile in JDK 11+.\n","numLines":103,"startLine":1,"totalLines":103}}}
